<span class="ct-node-label">UFO Identifications</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Most UFO or UAP reports do not begin with anything intrinsically mysterious. They begin when an observer or sensor detects something that cannot be identified at the time. Subsequent investigation often turns the “unidentified” object into an IFO — an identified flying object or phenomenon.</span>
</span>

<span class="ct-node-content">
<span class="ct-node-label">Range Ambiguity</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">Why Radar Can Put a Target at the Wrong Range</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">A delayed echo can be assigned to the wrong transmitted pulse, making a distant reflector appear at a falsely nearer range.</span>
</span>
